FILE: CLAUDE.md

# Project Instructions

Arabic (RTL) dashboard for first-year Internal Medicine residents at Aleppo
University Hospital. Static site — no build step, no backend (yet) — reading
live from public Google Sheets.

**Read [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) before changing anything.** It carries the full
conventions, the invariants that must not break, and the documentation protocol.
Quick map: [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) (code) ·
[DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md) (data semantics) ·
[docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md) (sheet columns, generated) ·
[docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md](docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md) (diagnosing bad data) ·
[docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md](docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md) (the server plan).

## Git workflow

- **Always run and test changes locally.** Verify in the local working tree before anything else.
- **Never push to GitHub automatically.** Do not run `git push` (or any command that publishes to the remote) unless the user explicitly asks for it in that moment. Committing locally when requested is fine; pushing is the user's call.

## Working rules for this repo

- **Diagnose before fixing data complaints.** `node tools/verify-data.mjs` says
  whether the sheet or the code is at fault. Most "wrong numbers" reports have
  been the sheet.
- **Verify before claiming done.** Data-layer change → `node tools/verify-data.mjs`
  (exit 0). UI change → serve the page (`python3 -m http.server 8000`), load it,
  confirm no console errors. Hard-refresh after editing files under `js/`.
- **Layering:** `js/core` → `js/data` → `js/domain` → `js/views` → `js/app.js`.
  A view never fetches; a parser never touches the DOM.
- **Never read a sheet cell by index.** Declare the column in `js/data/schema.js`,
  then run `node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs`.
- **The site must keep working both over http(s) and opened directly from disk**
  (`file://` relies on the JSONP fallback in `js/data/gviz-client.js`).
- **Never touch `localStorage` directly** — use `AUH.storage`.
- **Keep it Arabic and RTL**, and escape every sheet value that enters HTML.
- **Finish with the docs:** update the owning `.md` file(s) and append a
  CHANGELOG.md entry (date, who, what, files, "Docs synced").

# AGENTS.md — Guide for AI Assistants

This file follows the cross-tool [`AGENTS.md`](https://agents.md) convention and
is meant to be read by **any** AI coding assistant (Claude, GPT, Gemini, Llama,
Cursor, Copilot, etc.), not one specific product. If you are an AI model asked to
work on this repo, read this first, then [README.md](README.md),
[ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md), and [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md).

---

## ⚠️ Start-of-session and end-of-session protocol (do this every time)

1. **At the start of a session:** read [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) to see what
   previous sessions/models did, then this file and the other docs.
2. **Before trusting a bug report about wrong/missing data**, run
   `node tools/verify-data.mjs`. It answers "is this the code or the sheet?" in
   seconds, and it has been right more often than the report was.
3. **At the end of any change** (code *or* docs), you **must**:
   - Append an entry to [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) using the template there
     (newest first, real date, who you are, what changed, whether docs were synced).
   - If you changed behavior, a column contract, a tab, or the cache key, **update
     the affected `.md` file(s)** so the docs never drift from the code.
   - Re-run `node tools/verify-data.mjs` (expect exit 0) and, for UI changes,
     load the page and confirm the console is clean.

This logging discipline is the single most important rule in this repo: it is how
future models (of any vendor) stay oriented across sessions. See
[Documentation logging protocol](#documentation-logging-protocol) below for details.

---

## TL;DR for a model with zero context

- This repo is a thin HTML shell (`index.html`) plus `styles.css`, a layered
  `js/` folder, two Node tools and two background images. No framework, no
  build step, no package manager, no backend.
- It renders an **Arabic, right-to-left** dashboard of internal-medicine
  residents at Aleppo University Hospital.
- All data is read **live, client-side, from public Google Sheets** via the
  gviz API. The app never writes data. To change *content*, you change the
  *sheet*, not the code.
- Layers, in dependency order (each may only use the ones before it):
  `js/core` → `js/data` → `js/domain` → `js/views` → `js/app.js`.
  See [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) for the file-by-file map.
- The page must keep working **both** ways: served over http(s) *and* opened
  straight from disk (`file://`, where `fetch` to Google is blocked by CORS and
  the JSONP fallback in `js/data/gviz-client.js` takes over). Never remove that
  fallback, and never assume `fetch` is available for sheet data.
- Never touch `localStorage` directly — use `AUH.storage` (it cannot throw).
- **Columns are never addressed by position.** Every sheet field is declared in
  `js/data/schema.js` by the header names it may carry; `js/data/header-map.js`
  resolves them and reports what it could not find.
- `styles.css` is still condensed onto long lines — edit it with targeted
  replacements. The JavaScript is normal, readable, commented code.

---

## Invariants — do not break these

These were each a real bug at some point. Changing any of them requires a very
good reason and a CHANGELOG entry that says so explicitly.

| Invariant | Why |
|---|---|
| The page must work **both** served over http(s) **and** opened from disk (`file://`). | Google only sends CORS headers to a real origin; from `file://` every `fetch` fails and the page renders empty. The JSONP fallback in `js/data/gviz-client.js` is what keeps that case working. |
| Sheet columns are addressed **by name**, never by position. | Column order/naming changes constantly; positional reads produced silently wrong numbers for months. |
| A failed load must be **visible**, never a silently empty page. | The "site works but has no data" report came from exactly this. `reportLoadResult()` owns it. |
| An empty result is **never cached**. | Otherwise one failed load keeps the next visit empty too. |
| `localStorage` is only touched through `AUH.storage`. | A throw in private mode used to abort `init()` half-way, leaving a rendered but data-less page. |
| Every sheet value is **escaped** before it enters HTML. | The sheet is public and editable by several people. |
| Dates are ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` everywhere past the parsers. | Both transports and the cache must agree; display formatting happens at the edge (`formatDisplayDate`). |
| Arabic comparisons go through `normAr`-based helpers. | `أحمد` vs `احمد` must not be two different people. |
| Statistics stay **computed**, never read from a hand-maintained tab. | The manual tab was abandoned because it was always out of date. |
| A background refresh must never move the reader: same tab, same selected day/month, same scroll. | People read on-call lists on their phone while the timer ticks. |
| Only the visible tab is rendered; others are marked dirty and render when opened. | Rendering every tab on a timer is what made the UI stutter (~1.5 MB of HTML). |
| PNG exports render a dedicated layout (`views/capture-layouts.js`), not the live DOM. | The screen wants compact dots; the image wants the duty name spelled out. |
| Colours come from the tokens in `:root` / `body.dark-mode`, never hardcoded hexes. | The palette was unified once; a stray `#1b3a5c` breaks dark mode silently. |
| No decorative gradients, no glassmorphism, no colour-blur orbs, no shadow on cards. | These were removed on purpose — they are what made the site look machine-made. |
| No build step, no framework, no bundler. | Deliberate: anyone can edit a file and publish. |

---

## How to run / verify a change

There is nothing to compile.

```bash
python3 -m http.server 8000        # visit http://localhost:8000
node tools/verify-data.mjs         # data-contract check against the live sheets
node tools/verify-data.mjs --debug # + the resolved column maps
```

`tools/verify-data.mjs` loads the real `js/core`, `js/data` and `js/domain`
modules in Node (no DOM) and asserts that every required column resolved, every
name in the on-call sheet matches a resident, praise counts agree with the
evaluation sheet, and the computed totals add up. **Run it after any change to
the data layer, and whenever the numbers on the site look wrong** — it usually
tells you the sheet changed, not the code.

Then verify in the browser:
1. The loading screen disappears and the roster (لائحة المقيمين) populates.
2. Switch tabs across the top nav; each renders without console errors.
3. Toggle dark mode (moon icon, top-left in RTL).
4. Open DevTools console — no thrown exceptions, no `[schema]` errors.
   `localStorage.setItem('auh_debug','1')` turns on verbose parsing logs.

Because data comes from live external sheets, "it loads real data" depends on
network + sheet permissions, not on your code changes alone.

---

## Editing safely — read before you touch code

1. **Put code in the right layer.** A parser never touches the DOM; a view never
   fetches or reaches into `AUH.data.*` / `AUH.domain.*` directly (it calls a
   facade method on `HospitalApp`). Adding a file means adding a `<script>` to
   the ordered list in `index.html` — order is dependency order.

2. **Sheet columns are a contract, declared once.** If a column was renamed or
   moved, edit `js/data/schema.js` (add the new name to `labels`) — never
   hard-code an index in a parser or view. Then regenerate the docs:
   `node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs`.

3. **Everything is Arabic and RTL.** Keep new user-facing strings in Arabic and
   consistent with the existing tone. Preserve `dir="rtl"`. "left" and "right"
   in CSS are visually swapped.

4. **`window.app` global + inline handlers.** Rendered HTML uses
   `onclick="app.method(...)"`. If you rename a method on `HospitalApp` (or on a
   view mixin), update every inline handler string that calls it. The only other
   intentional globals are the handful in `js/core/ui-kit.js`
   (`toggleDarkMode`, `copyPhone`, `toggleCollapsible`, …).

5. **HTML is built by string concatenation and injected with `innerHTML`.**
   Sheet values must be escaped: `this.escapeHtml(value)` in views (or
   `AUH.text.escapeHtml`), and `AUH.text.escapeJsString` for values interpolated
   into an inline handler. Do not add a new unescaped interpolation point.

6. **Compare Arabic text with `normAr`-based helpers**, never with `===`:
   `exactNameMatch` for joins, `smartSearch` for search boxes. Raw equality
   silently fails on أ/ا, ة/ه and "ال".

7. **Dates are ISO strings** (`YYYY-MM-DD`) everywhere past the parsers. Parse
   sheet dates with `AUH.dates.extractDate` and keep them ISO.

8. **No dependencies to add casually.** New third-party code would mean another
   CDN `<script>`/`<link>`. Prefer vanilla JS. Existing CDN deps: Font Awesome
   (icons), Tajawal/Changa (fonts), `html2canvas` (image export), SheetJS (Excel).

9. **Cache versioning.** If a change makes old cached data incompatible, bump
   `cacheVersion` in `js/core/config.js` (`hc_v64` → `hc_v65`).

10. **Logging goes through `AUH.log`.** `warn`/`error` are always shown (they
    mean someone should look at the sheet); `debug`/`info` only appear with
    `localStorage.setItem('auh_debug','1')`. Do not ship bare `console.log`.

## Where things live (quick index)

| You want to… | Go to |
|---|---|
| Change colors/theme | `styles.css` — `:root` variables and `body.dark-mode`. |
| Add/rename a tab | `TABS` in `js/core/config.js` + the markup in `js/views/layout.js` + a render hook in `renderTab()` (`js/app.js`). |
| Fix a renamed/moved sheet column | `js/data/schema.js` — nothing else. |
| Change how residents are parsed | `js/data/parsers/residents.js`. |
| Change on-call parsing (either year) | `js/data/parsers/oncall.js`. |
| Change praise/evaluation logic | `js/data/parsers/evaluation.js`. |
| Change duty times, holidays, groups | `js/domain/oncall-schedule.js`. |
| Change the computed statistics | `js/domain/doctor-stats.js`. |
| Change search behavior | `js/core/text.js` (`normAr` / `smartSearch` / `exactNameMatch`). |
| Change the on-call calendar / day view | `js/views/oncall.js`. |
| Change "معلوماتي" | `js/views/myinfo.js`. |
| Change image/Excel/contact export | `js/views/exports.js`, `js/views/residents.js` (vCard). |
| Change the data source, transport or intervals | `js/core/config.js`. |
| Point the site at a backend | set `apiBaseUrl` in `js/core/config.js` (`dataSource: 'auto'` does the rest). |
| Debug "no data is showing" | run `app.diagnose()` in the console, then `node tools/verify-data.mjs`. |
| Move to a server/database | `js/data/repository.js` + [docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md](docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md). |

## What NOT to do

- ❌ Don't add a build system, bundler, or framework. The static, no-build
  nature is a deliberate constraint (easy hosting, easy hand-editing).
- ❌ Don't move data into the repo or hardcode resident data — it belongs in the
  Google Sheet so non-developers can maintain it.
- ❌ Don't reorder or "clean up" Google Sheet columns to match prettier code;
  the sheet is maintained by other people and the indices are load-bearing.
- ❌ Don't commit secrets — there are none here, and none are needed (the sheet
  is public-read).
- ❌ Don't reflow/reformat a whole file as a side effect of a small change.
- ❌ Don't read a sheet cell by a hard-coded index. Declare it in the schema.
- ❌ Don't put business logic in a view or DOM code in a parser.

---

## Documentation logging protocol

The docs in this repo are only useful to future models if they stay **accurate**
and **current**. Enforce that with this protocol on every session:

### The doc set and what each owns
| File | Owns (keep it accurate when you touch the matching thing) |
|---|---|
| [README.md](README.md) | What the app is, the tab list, features, how to run and verify it. Update if you add/remove a feature or tab. |
| [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) | Code structure: the layers, the load pipeline, the transports, the view mixins, app state. Update if you add/rename/move a module or change the data flow. |
| [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md) | How the sheets are read, what the app computes (statistics, schedules, adjustments), caching. Update when parsing or computation semantics change. |
| [docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md) | **Generated** from `js/data/schema.js`. Never edit by hand — run `node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs`. |
| [docs/OVERVIEW-AR.md](docs/OVERVIEW-AR.md) | Arabic, non-developer facing: what the site is, what each tab shows, where data comes from, who maintains what, privacy. Update when the product changes, not when the code moves. |
| [docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md](docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md) | Arabic: how to diagnose "no data / wrong numbers", plus known sheet-side data problems. Update when you find a new failure mode or fix a listed one. |
| [docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md](docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md) | Arabic: the server/database plan and the exact seam (`dataSource`, `apiBaseUrl`, `api-client.js`). Update if the data layer's seams change. |
| [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) | This guide. Update if you change a convention, an invariant, or add a constraint future models must follow. |
| [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) | The append-only history. **Always** add an entry. |
| `project.txt` | **Generated** single-file bundle of the codebase for feeding to an AI. Regenerate with `node tools/bundle-project.mjs` after structural changes. |

### Required steps for any change
1. Make the code/doc change.
2. **Sync the owning doc(s)** from the table above so they match reality.
3. **Append a CHANGELOG.md entry** (newest first) using the template in that
   file. State the real date, who you are (model/tool name), what changed, the
   files touched, and **"Docs synced: yes/no"** with the list of `.md` files you
   updated.
4. If you bumped the cache key (`CK`), said the column contract changed, or
   renamed inline-handler methods, call that out explicitly in the entry — those
   have downstream impact.

### Style for changelog/doc entries (so any model can parse them)
- Plain Markdown, short factual sentences, no tool-specific jargon.
- Newest entry on top; never edit or delete past entries — append a correction
  entry instead.
- Prefer stable identifier names over line numbers when referencing code (line
  numbers drift).
- Write dates as `YYYY-MM-DD`.

### Self-check before you finish
- [ ] Did behavior or the data contract change? → owning `.md` updated.
- [ ] CHANGELOG.md entry appended with date, author, and "Docs synced" line.
- [ ] No past changelog entries were modified or removed.
- [ ] Line numbers I added are marked approximate (or I used identifier names).

---

## Good first tasks (examples)

- Show a sheet column the app already parses but does not display (e.g.
  `الجنس`, `الجامعة الأم`, `مناوبات شهر N`) — the field is already in the
  schema and on the resident record.
- Add a new tab: `TABS` (`js/core/config.js`) → markup in `js/views/layout.js`
  → a view file → a hook in `renderTab()`.
- Add a check to `tools/verify-data.mjs` for a data mistake that bit someone.
- Improve accessibility (ARIA labels, focus states) without changing behavior.

When in doubt about data shape, read [docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md)
and [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md); when in doubt about code structure, read
[ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md). When the numbers look wrong, run
`node tools/verify-data.mjs` before touching any code.

================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================
# AUH Internal Medicine — Residents Dashboard

A single-page web app: a thin `index.html` shell plus `styles.css` and a layered
`js/` folder (core → data → domain → views). It displays information about
**first-year Internal Medicine residents at Aleppo University Hospital**
(مشفى حلب الجامعي / قسم الداخلية - السنة الأولى).

The UI is **Arabic and right-to-left (RTL)**. All data is read **live from a
public Google Sheet** — there is no backend server and no database. The page is
pure static HTML/CSS/JavaScript and can be hosted on any static host (GitHub
Pages, Netlify, Cloudflare Pages, a plain file server, etc.).

> **Audience note:** This README and the companion docs ([ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md),
> [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md), [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md)) are written to be read by
> **any AI model or assistant**, not just one specific tool. They describe the
> project from scratch so a model with no prior context can understand and safely
> modify it.

---

## Documentation map

| Read this | When |
|---|---|
| **[docs/OVERVIEW-AR.md](docs/OVERVIEW-AR.md)** (عربي) | You want to know what the site *is*: every tab, where the data comes from, what the app computes itself, privacy, and who maintains what. Written for non-developers too. |
| **[docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md](docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md)** (عربي) | Something looks empty or wrong. Start here — it separates sheet problems from code problems in two commands. |
| **[docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md)** (عربي, generated) | You are editing a Google Sheet: exact column names the app accepts, per tab. |
| [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) | You are changing code: layers, load pipeline, transports, view mixins, app state. |
| [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md) | You need the data semantics: column resolution, canonical on-call shape, computed statistics, schedules, caching. |
| [docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md](docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md) (عربي) | You are moving to a server + database: the exact seam, a proposed SQL schema and REST API. |
| [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) | You are an AI assistant (or a new developer): conventions, invariants, and the mandatory doc/changelog protocol. |
| [CHANGELOG.md](CHANGELOG.md) | You want to know what changed, when, and why. |

---

## What it does

The app presents resident data across **11 primary tabs** (Arabic label → meaning):

| Tab id | Arabic label | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `residents` | لائحة المقيمين | Full roster: name, specialty, phone, status, shift. Search, filter, multi-select, export to phone contacts (vCard). |
| `lectures` | رزنامة المحاضرات | Medical lectures/activities calendar with smart search, category/department/year filters, today highlights, upcoming list, optional old-events view, and optional registration/announcement links. |
| `shifts` | الفروز | Monthly "shift assignment" (فرز) groups — which residents are assigned where, per month. |
| `oncall` | المناوبات | On-call schedule. Monthly calendar + per-day breakdown by category, with a year filter (أولى فقط / ثانية فقط / أولى + ثانية) that also pulls in a second, structurally different Year-2 spreadsheet. Exportable as an image. |
| `exams` | الامتحانات والاختبارات | Placeholder page for upcoming exams/tests features (work in progress). |
| `clinicalcases` | مشروع الحالات السريرية | Placeholder page for clinical-cases project features (work in progress). |
| `doctorstats` | احصائيات الأطباء | Doctor analytics **computed by the app** (not read from a sheet tab): days since join, cumulative vs. completed on-calls, ward/ICU/emergency/misc breakdown, holiday/night counts, and total hours — shown as cards with smart search and sorting by hours. |
| `evaluation` | التقييم السنوي | Annual evaluation scores per resident across 8 skill areas, plus praises (ثناءات) and penalties (عقوبات). |
| `links` | روابط هامة | Important links / channels (e.g. group chats, resources). |
| `myinfo` | معلوماتي | "My info" — search yourself by name/abbreviation and see a personal summary card with an identity header (name, abbreviation, sequence number), static cumulative/join-day counters, cumulative on-call distribution, extra resident info, evaluation, shift data, computed doctor statistics, and a month-selectable on-call calendar. Each on-call entry lists Year-1 colleagues in the same shift plus, where a matching category exists, Year-2 colleagues on that same date. On-call days jump to their cards, past days/cards are visually marked as completed, and holiday shifts are distinguished without recoloring the whole card. Exportable as an image. |
| `qa` | Q&A (الأسئلة والأجوبة) | Categorized, collapsible frequently-asked questions. |

A separate floating **الشكاوي** support shortcut (outside the primary tab row)
opens the technical-contact view when needed.

### Notable features
- **Schema-driven sheet reading**: one declarative contract (`js/data/schema.js`) maps
  Arabic header names to the fields the app needs, so renaming/moving/inserting a
  column in the sheet no longer breaks anything silently — unresolved columns are
  reported instead. Verify any time with `node tools/verify-data.mjs`.
- **Live Google Sheets data** via the gviz endpoint (CSV + JSON), refreshed every 3 minutes,
  with an automatic JSONP fallback so the page also works opened straight from disk.
- **Ready for a backend without losing the sheets**: `dataSource: 'auto'` uses the
  JSON API once `apiBaseUrl` is set and falls back to the sheets if it is unreachable.
- **Loud failures**: if data cannot be loaded, a clear Arabic message with a retry
  button appears instead of a silent, empty page — and an empty result is never cached.
- **Quiet refresh**: a background refresh compares a content signature first — if no
  sheet changed, nothing is parsed, nothing re-renders, nothing moves on screen. When
  something did change, the visitor keeps their tab, selected on-call day, month and
  scroll position, and the "Last update" badge turns into a red **New data — tap to
  refresh** reminder that the manual refresh clears.
- **Fast tabs**: a tab renders when it is opened and only when its data is stale, so
  re-opening a rendered tab costs ~1 ms instead of rebuilding ~780 KB of HTML.
- **PNG exports for "معلوماتي"**: the month calendar alone, or the calendar plus that
  month's on-call details — rendered from a dedicated print layout (duty name spelled out
  under each green day, date and time stamped at the top), never a screenshot of the UI.
- **Month arrows** on both the on-call and "معلوماتي" calendars.
- **English header badges**: `Sunday, 23 آب 2026` and `Last update 7:44 PM` (Latin
  numerals, Levantine month names).
- **Offline-ish caching** in `localStorage` (10-minute TTL) so the page renders instantly from cache, then refreshes in the background.
- **Strong non-disruptive refresh**: background updates fetch from network with `no-store` + cache-busting, while preserving user context (especially selected on-call date) to avoid jumping to today.
- **Deployment cache busting**: each GitHub Pages deploy now injects a unique build id into `index.html`, appends it to local asset URLs (`styles.css`, every file under `js/`, images), and exposes it via `<meta name="app-build">`.
- **Automatic client update**: running clients periodically fetch the latest `index.html` with `no-store`; when a newer build id is detected, stale local app caches are cleared and the page reloads to the latest version.
- **Doctor statistics tab** computed directly by the app from the residents roster + the on-call log (no separate hand-maintained sheet tab), shown as cards with group breakdowns, holiday/night counts, and total hours.
- **Bonus hours**: a row in the adjustments sheet whose date/type says `Bonus` credits
  hours to a resident without inventing an on-call shift — shown as a separate figure on
  the statistics card, in the "معلوماتي" summary, per month, and in the Excel export,
  while still counting toward the hours ranking.
- **Excel export** now includes the praise count, the number of rotations and one column
  per `فرز شهر N`, so rotations are filterable in the spreadsheet.
- **Official holidays from their own sheet tab** (date + name): every listed day becomes a
  holiday everywhere — holiday duty times and durations (so the computed hours change),
  the holiday counter, distinct colouring plus the holiday's name in both calendars, a
  dedicated "العطل الرسمية" panel in معلوماتي, and the exported image.
- **On-call timing rules** sourced from a dedicated sheet tab to show duty times/durations and holiday-aware highlights (Fri/Sat + annual holidays from sheet).
- **My Info on-call UX upgrade**: the personal card now uses a cleaner identity header, static cumulative/join-day cards, an always-visible cumulative distribution panel, a month dropdown for on-calls, cleaner month statistics, and a denser month calendar with on-call labels inside each active day.
- **My Info completed/holiday styling**: finished on-calls are highlighted in green with a clear `تم` badge, while holiday on-calls keep the normal card color and only use a red dot / `عطلة` badge.
- **On-call raw table freeze polish**: sticky header row and first two sticky columns were refined to avoid spacing artifacts while scrolling.
- **Lectures calendar polish**: month navigation now uses dedicated arrow buttons, the month title is shown as `الشهر X`, only the currently selected day is filled, and clicking an empty day keeps that day selected while showing `لا يوجد.` under the correct day/date heading.
- **Dark mode** toggle (persisted in `localStorage`).
- **Arabic-aware search** with normalization (handles أ/إ/آ/ا, ة/ه, ى/ي, the "ال" prefix, diacritics, etc.).
- **Export to contacts**: select residents → download a `.vcf` (vCard) file.
- **Detached-status handling**: residents with status `تم الانفكاك` are hidden from the roster by default, excluded from contact export, and can be shown using a small side filter button.
- **Export to image**: render the on-call card or "my info" card to a PNG using `html2canvas`; on-call export now uses a cleaner compact capture layout with two download options (normal and high quality) so users can choose sharper output when needed.
- **Shift month auto-preference**: if next month's shift column already has real values, the shifts views default to it immediately (instead of waiting for calendar month rollover).
- **Shift month control polish**: month selector in the shifts tab now has clearer modern styling and better spacing.
- **Faster first header paint**: header background image is now eagerly loaded to reduce partial/flicker appearance on mobile.
- **Click-to-copy phone numbers** via tooltips.
- **Responsive**: desktop tables collapse into mobile cards under 768px.
- **Lectures calendar UX**: large "today" highlight cards for sessions not yet finished, separate upcoming sessions list, and a toggle to reveal past sessions.
- **Interactive lectures month calendar**: month grid now highlights days with lectures in green (with counts) and shows that day's lectures when clicked.
- **Flexible lectures date parsing**: lecture dates now accept multiple input styles (including `YYYY-MM-DD`, `DD/MM/YYYY`, `MM/DD/YYYY`, Arabic/English month names, and `Date(y,m,d)` gviz-style values).

---

## Quick start

There is **no build step and no dependencies to install.** The app still runs
as plain static files.

**Either way works:**

```bash
# 1) just open it
open index.html

# 2) or serve it (recommended while developing)
python3 -m http.server 8000   # then visit http://localhost:8000
```

Opening the file directly (`file://`) works because the app falls back to a
JSONP (`<script>`) request when the browser blocks `fetch`: Google only sends
CORS headers to a real http(s) origin, so from `file://` a normal fetch returns
nothing at all. See "Data transports" in [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md).

> While developing over a local server, do a **hard refresh** (⇧⌘R / Ctrl-F5)
> after editing a file under `js/` — the asset URLs only change per deploy, so a
> normal reload can serve a cached mix of old and new files.

If a tab looks empty, open the console and run `app.diagnose()` — it reports the
transport used, what failed to load, and how many records each area holds. The
full diagnosis routine is in [docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md](docs/TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md).

### Maintenance commands

```bash
node tools/verify-data.mjs             # is the data (and the sheet) still sound?
node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs  # after editing js/data/schema.js
node tools/bundle-project.mjs          # after structural changes, refresh project.txt
```

Check the data itself (no browser needed, requires Node 18+):

```bash
node tools/verify-data.mjs
```

It runs the app's real parsers and statistics against the live sheets and reports
renamed columns, unreadable dates, names that cannot be matched between sheets,
and praise counts that do not add up.

Third-party libraries (Font Awesome icons, the Tajawal Arabic font, and
`html2canvas`) are loaded from CDNs at runtime, so an internet connection is
required for the full experience — and is required regardless, since the data
itself comes from Google Sheets.

### Files in the repo
| File / folder | Role |
|---|---|
| `index.html` | Thin HTML shell: head, header, containers, and the ordered `<script>` list. |
| `styles.css` | All app styling. |
| `js/core/` | Config and pure helpers (Arabic text, dates, status vocabulary, safe storage, UI kit). |
| `js/data/` | The data contract: schema, header resolution, transports (sheets + future API), parsers, repository. |
| `js/domain/` | Business rules: duty schedules, holidays, adjustments, computed statistics. |
| `js/views/` | One file per tab, mixed into the app prototype. |
| `js/app.js` | `HospitalApp` — lifecycle, tabs, refresh, view facade, bootstrap. |
| `tools/verify-data.mjs` | Runs the real parsers against the live sheets and reports problems (Node). |
| `tools/generate-data-contract.mjs` | Regenerates `docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md` from the schema. |
| `tools/bundle-project.mjs` | Regenerates `project.txt`, the single-file source bundle for feeding to an AI. |
| `docs/` | Arabic-facing documentation: overview, troubleshooting, sheet contract, backend plan. |
| `.github/workflows/pages.yml` | GitHub Pages deployment (copies `js/` and injects the build id). |
| `bg.webp` / `bg.png` | Header background image (the `.webp` is used; `.png` is a fallback/source). |
| `_legacy/` | Pre-refactor snapshot of the old single-file `app.js` / `helpers.js`. Safe to delete. |
| `docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md` | Generated column-by-column sheet contract (Arabic). |
| `docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md` | Plan for moving to a server + database (Arabic). |
| `README.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, `DATA-MODEL.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` | Documentation. |
| `project.txt` | Generated single-file bundle of the codebase for AI use — refresh with `node tools/bundle-project.mjs`. |

### GitHub Pages deployment mode

- This repo uses **GitHub Actions** deployment for Pages via [`.github/workflows/pages.yml`](.github/workflows/pages.yml).
- In repository settings, set **Pages > Source** to **GitHub Actions**.
- If source is set to branch mode while relying on Actions checks, deployment checks can fail quickly.
- The workflow publishes a clean `_site` artifact (not the whole repo root), containing only runtime site files, and injects a per-deploy build id into `index.html` for cache-safe asset URLs.
- The deploy job retries up to 3 times to handle transient Pages backend errors like `Deployment failed, try again later`.

---

## How the data flows (one paragraph)

On load, `HospitalApp` reads any cached snapshot from `localStorage` (key
`hc_v64_<buildId>`) and renders it immediately, then fetches fresh data from the Google
Sheet in the background. Seven sheet tabs are fetched in parallel by their
**GID** (numeric tab id): residents, on-call, evaluation, links, Q&A,
lectures calendar, and on-call rules (used only for annual holiday dates now — see
[DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md)). Doctor statistics are **computed by the app**
from the residents + on-call data, not read from a sheet tab. CSV
tabs are parsed by a hand-written CSV parser; JSON tabs use the gviz JSON
response. The parsed rows are stored on the app instance and rendered into the
relevant tab. A `setInterval` re-fetches every 120 seconds (network-first),
and refresh on tab resume uses the same non-disruptive path without forcing a
full page reload. See
[DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md) for the exact sheet/column layout and
[ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) for the code structure.

---

## Editing the content vs. editing the code

- **To change displayed data** (residents, schedules, lectures, evaluations, links, Q&A):
  edit the **Google Sheet** — not this repo. The sheet id and tab GIDs are in
  [DATA-MODEL.md](DATA-MODEL.md). The site picks up changes within ~2 minutes
  (or immediately on a hard refresh that bypasses cache).
- **To change layout, styling, or behavior:** edit the matching file under
  `js/` (see [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) for the map) or `styles.css`.
- **If a sheet column was renamed or moved:** update `js/data/schema.js` only —
  nothing else reads column positions. Then run `node tools/verify-data.mjs` and
  `node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs`.

---

## Important constraints / gotchas

- The Google Sheet **must be publicly readable** ("anyone with the link can
  view") for the gviz endpoints to work from the browser. If data stops
  loading, sharing permissions are the first thing to check.
- **Every** sheet is now read by header name, not by column position, and the
  accepted names are declared in one place (`js/data/schema.js`). Monthly
  columns (`فرز شهر N`, `مناوبات شهر N`) and on-call category columns are
  discovered automatically. A column the app cannot find is reported in the
  console instead of silently producing wrong numbers.
  See [docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md).
- The cache key is **versioned** (`cacheVersion` in `js/core/config.js`, today
  `hc_v64`). Bumping it invalidates everyone's cached data — useful after a
  breaking data change.
- All user-facing strings are Arabic. Keep RTL and Arabic copy consistent when
  editing.

FILE: ARCHITECTURE.md
================================================================
# Architecture & Code Guide

How the app is organized internally, so any developer or AI model can navigate
and change it confidently. The runtime is still a **static, no-build** single
page app: `index.html` loads plain `<script defer>` files in dependency order.

> Read [docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md) alongside this — most of
> the data layer only makes sense once you know the sheet contract it enforces.
> For the planned server/database version, see
> [docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md](docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md).

---

## File layout

```
index.html          thin shell: head, header, nav/main containers, script tags
styles.css          all styling
js/
  core/             config + pure helpers (no DOM, no data knowledge)
    namespace.js      the single global: window.AUH
    config.js         ids, GIDs, intervals, cache key, data source + transport
    storage.js        localStorage that never throws (private mode, quota, file://)
    logger.js         AUH.log — warn/error always, detail only when debugging
    text.js           Arabic normalization, search, escaping, numbers
    status.js         "الحالة" vocabulary (joined / detached / …)
    dates.js          ISO date helpers, Arabic day names, gviz date parsing
    ui-kit.js         dark mode, toasts, tooltip, collapsibles + the few globals
                      that inline onclick="" handlers need
  data/             the Google Sheet contract
    schema.js         ONE declarative description of every sheet + column
    header-map.js     header names → column indexes, with diagnostics
    gviz-client.js    Google Sheets transport: fetch (CSV/JSON) + JSONP fallback
    api-client.js     the future JSON backend, same contract, inert until configured
    parsers/          raw table → domain model (residents, oncall, evaluation,
                      lectures, holidays, content)
    repository.js     fetch all → parse all → dataset, plus the localStorage cache
  domain/           business rules, pure functions
    oncall-schedule.js  fixed duty times/durations, holidays, group classification
    oncall-adjustments.js  overrides vs. volunteer additions
    doctor-stats.js     the computed statistics
  views/            DOM rendering, one file per tab, mixed into the app prototype
    layout.js         nav + tab markup
    residents.js shifts.js oncall.js lectures.js evaluation.js
    links-qa.js doctor-stats.js myinfo.js
    capture-layouts.js  print-quality HTML for the PNG exports
    exports.js        rasterizer (html2canvas) + Excel export
  app.js            HospitalApp: lifecycle, tabs, refresh, view facade, bootstrap
tools/
  verify-data.mjs             runs the real parsers against the live sheets (Node)
  generate-data-contract.mjs  regenerates docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md from the schema
  bundle-project.mjs          regenerates project.txt (whole codebase in one file)
docs/
  OVERVIEW-AR.md              what the site is, per tab, for non-developers (عربي)
  TROUBLESHOOTING-AR.md       diagnosing empty/wrong data (عربي)
  DATA-CONTRACT.md            generated sheet-column contract (عربي)
  BACKEND-MIGRATION.md        server + database plan (عربي)
_legacy/            pre-refactor snapshot of the old single-file app.js/helpers.js
project.txt         generated single-file bundle of the codebase, for AI use
```

**Layering rule:** a layer may only use the layers above it.
`views → domain → data → core`. No view fetches, no parser touches the DOM.

---

## Load pipeline

```
app.loadData()
  ├─ repository.readCache()  → render immediately if fresh (< 10 min)
  └─ app.loadFresh(silent)
       ├─ repository.fetchAll()      9 sources in parallel (gviz)
       ├─ repository.parseAll(raw)   → dataset of models
       └─ app.applyDataset(dataset)
            ├─ expose models + flat aliases (this.res, this.oncRows, …)
            ├─ domain.adjustments.resolveAdjustments()
            ├─ domain.doctorStats.computeDoctorStats()
            └─ app.renderAll()
```

A source that fails to load keeps its previous table (`app.rawTables`), so one
flaky request never blanks a tab. The cache stores the **raw tables**, which is
why a cached start renders exactly what a fresh start renders — and an empty
result is never written to the cache, so a failed load cannot poison the next
visit. When sources do fail, `reportLoadResult()` shows an Arabic message with a
retry button (`#dataErrorBar`) instead of leaving a silent, empty page.

Refresh happens: every 180 s while the tab is visible, on tab re-focus, and via
the "Last update" badge (`manualRefresh()`). A separate 90 s timer compares the
deployed build id in `index.html` with the running one and reloads on change.

### Rendering model (why the UI feels instant)

- `repository.tablesSignature()` hashes the fetched tables. A background refresh
  whose signature matches the current one **stops there**: no parsing, no
  statistics, no DOM work, nothing moves under the reader.
- When the data did change, `applyDataset()` recomputes and calls `renderAll()`,
  which marks every tab dirty but re-renders **only the tab on screen**, keeping
  the scroll position. Other tabs render when opened (`renderTab` skips clean
  tabs, so re-opening one costs ~1 ms).
- Context is preserved across refreshes: active tab, selected on-call date and
  month, "معلوماتي" resident and month, filters and search terms.
- New data raises the red `New data — tap to refresh` badge
  (`markNewData()` / `clearNewData()`); `manualRefresh()` forces a full
  re-render — including re-running `showMe()` for the searched resident — and
  clears the badge.

---

## Data transports (why there are two)

`AUH.config.dataSource` decides where data comes from:

| value | behaviour |
|---|---|
| `'auto'` (default) | the JSON API when `apiBaseUrl` is set, otherwise Google Sheets — and the sheets as a fallback if the API fails |
| `'gviz'` | always Google Sheets |
| `'api'` | always the JSON backend |

Within Google Sheets, `AUH.config.sheetsTransport` decides *how*:

| value | behaviour |
|---|---|
| `'auto'` (default) | normal `fetch`, falling back to JSONP when it is blocked |
| `'fetch'` | fetch only |
| `'jsonp'` | JSONP only (useful for testing the fallback) |

**Why the fallback exists:** Google echoes the page's `Origin` back in
`Access-Control-Allow-Origin`. A page opened straight from disk has the origin
`null`, gets no CORS header, and every `fetch` fails — the page renders perfectly
and contains no data. A `<script>` tag is not subject to CORS, so the JSONP path
(`tqx=out:json;responseHandler:CB`) keeps working there.

Both transports are normalized to the identical table shape; the JSON path also
promotes the first row to the header when gviz reports no column labels, and
converts dates to ISO and numbers to their formatted text (so a phone number
keeps its leading zero). Verified: `fetch` and `jsonp` produce byte-identical
results for every tab.

---

## The data layer (why it looks like this)

The sheets are edited by people: columns get renamed, reordered and inserted.
The old code read most cells by fixed position, so those edits silently produced
wrong numbers. Now:

1. **`data/schema.js`** declares, per sheet, the fields the app needs and the
   header names each may appear under.
2. **`data/header-map.js`** resolves them: exact → prefix → unambiguous
   contains → declared fallback position. Every column is claimed once, and
   anything unresolved is reported (`console.error` for required fields).
3. **`data/parsers/*`** turn raw tables into models with named fields and small
   query methods (`getShift`, `getPreferredShiftMonth`, `findByNameOrAbbr`,
   `namesFor`, `findFor` …).

Both on-call sheets are normalized into the **same canonical shape**, regardless
of their physical layout:

```
headers: ['اليوم', 'التاريخ', ...categories]
rows:    [{ date: 'YYYY-MM-DD', day: 'الأحد', row: [day, date, ...cells] }]
```

The Year-2 sheet's header layout (one row of repeated labels vs. two merged
rows) is **detected at runtime** by checking whether row 1 starts with a date —
that is what kept it working when the sheet was restructured in August 2026.

---

## The domain layer

| Module | Owns |
|---|---|
| `oncall-schedule.js` | `ONCALL_SCHEDULE_OLD/NEW` + `SWITCH_DATE`, `isHolidayDate`, `classifyGroup`, `isNightCategory`, `year2CategoriesFor` |
| `oncall-adjustments.js` | classifies each adjustment row as an hours **override** or a volunteer **addition** |
| `doctor-stats.js` | `computeDoctorStats(ctx)` — one pass over the on-call log producing counts, group breakdowns with dates, holiday/night counts, hours + ranks, praise count and rotations |

All three are pure functions over parsed models: no DOM, no globals, no `this`.
That is what lets `tools/verify-data.mjs` run them in Node against the live
sheets, and what makes them reusable unchanged once a server provides the data.

---

## The view layer

Each `js/views/*.js` exports an object of methods that is mixed into
`HospitalApp.prototype` at the bottom of `js/app.js`:

```js
Object.assign(HospitalApp.prototype, AUH.views.residents, AUH.views.shifts, …);
```

So `app.showOncallDate(...)` still works from an inline `onclick=""`, while the
code lives in a focused file. Views read `this.*` state and call the **facade**
methods on the app (`this.findRbyExact`, `this.isHolidayDate`,
`this.getCategorySchedule`, `this.getEvalForResident`, `this.escapeHtml`,
`this.formatNumDisplay`, …) rather than reaching into the data/domain modules.

| View | Renders |
|---|---|
| `residents.js` | roster table + cards, filters, selection, vCard export |
| `shifts.js` | الفروز — rotation groups for a chosen month |
| `oncall.js` | month calendar, per-day breakdown, raw-table modal, year filter |
| `lectures.js` | lectures calendar, selected day, upcoming/past lists |
| `evaluation.js` | evaluation table + cards (schema-driven columns) |
| `links-qa.js` | links list and grouped Q&A |
| `doctor-stats.js` | statistics cards, sort panel, search |
| `myinfo.js` | the personal card (the most complex renderer) |
| `capture-layouts.js` | Self-contained, inline-styled HTML for the exported images (calendar with duty names under each day, detail rows, header band with timestamp) |
| `exports.js` | Rasterizes those layouts (`captureNode`) or a live card (`captureElement`) to PNG, plus the Excel export (SheetJS) |

---

## App state (fields the views read)

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `this.m`, `this.today` | current month index, today as `YYYY-MM-DD` |
| `this.dataset` | the parsed dataset (all models) |
| `this.residentsModel` / `this.res` | residents model / its records array |
| `this.oncallModel`, `this.oncall2Model` | canonical on-call models |
| `this.oncHeaders`, `this.oncRows` (+`2`) | flat aliases of those models |
| `this.evaluationModel`, `this.linksModel`, `this.qaModel`, `this.lectures` | other tabs' data |
| `this.annualHolidays` | `Set` of holiday dates from the rules tab |
| `this.adjustmentOverrides`, `this.adjustmentAdditions` | resolved adjustments |
| `this.doctorStats` | computed statistics |
| filters/search/month/selection fields | per-tab UI state (see the constructor) |

---

## Design language — "دفتر المناوبات" (the duty ledger)

The visual direction comes from the object this app replaces: the duty roster
taped to the ward wall. Paper, ink, hairline rules, and a stamp.

| Token | Light | Dark | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--paper` | `#f6f4ef` | `#0a0b0c` | page background (warm paper / true black) |
| `--surface` | `#ffffff` | `#131518` | panels and cards |
| `--surface-sunk` | `#efece5` | `#1a1d21` | inputs, table headers, chips |
| `--ink` / `--ink-2` | `#15181b` / `#59616b` | `#eae7e1` / `#99a1aa` | text |
| `--rule` | `#ddd8cd` | `#262a2f` | every border — 1px, no shadows |
| `--primary` | `#14563f` | `#4ca987` | duty green: the one emphasis colour |
| `--signal` | `#a62b22` | `#e0655a` | holidays, weekends, errors — nothing else |
| `--ochre` | `#9a6b15` | `#d3a24a` | things that were *earned*: praise, Bonus |

Rules that keep it from drifting back into a template:

- **One family**: IBM Plex Sans Arabic (400/500/600/700), with
  `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` everywhere numbers are compared. Hours,
  dates and counts are the content — they line up in columns.
- **Radius language**: `12px` panels, `8px` controls, `999px` pills, `0` table
  cells. Nothing else.
- **Structure comes from rules, not shadows.** `--shadow` is `none`; elevation
  exists only for things that genuinely float (modal, dropdown, tooltip, toast).
- **No gradients as decoration.** The only gradient left is the veil that makes
  the header photograph readable.
- **The photograph is the identity** — the real building, desaturated behind a
  dark veil, with a green hairline under it. No stock imagery, no blurred colour
  orbs, no glassmorphism.
- **The signature element is the calendar day cell**: green fill = duty, red
  stamp = official holiday with its name, `✓` = completed. It is what people
  open the site for, so it is where the colour goes.

Dark mode is genuinely dark (`#0a0b0c`), not navy: the app is read at 2 a.m. on
a phone in a lit ward.

Two surfaces carry the photograph and must stay that way:

- **The loading screen** (`.loading-screen`) is the building behind a dark veil,
  with the percentage set large in tabular numerals over it. It covers the page
  completely (`position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10000`) — if its `background`
  declaration ever breaks, the screen turns transparent and the half-built UI
  shows through, which is exactly the bug it was rebuilt to fix.
- **The masthead** (`.main-header`) is sized with
  `min-height: clamp(300px, 38vh, 430px)` (≥1200px: `clamp(340px,40vh,470px)`;
  ≤768px: `clamp(230px,34vh,300px)`), so the photo has room on a laptop and on a
  phone without pushing the content off screen. The title uses
  `clamp(1.55em, 5.6vw, 2.7em)` so it stays on two lines at 375px.


## Conventions

- **Arabic + RTL everywhere.** New user-facing strings stay Arabic and match the
  existing tone. "left"/"right" in CSS are visually swapped.
- **All sheet values are escaped** before being interpolated into HTML
  (`escapeHtml` / `escapeJsString`) — the sheet is not a trusted source.
- **Dates are ISO strings** (`YYYY-MM-DD`) everywhere past the parsers.
- **Names are joined via `normAr`-based matching**, never raw string equality.
- **Logging goes through `AUH.log`**; verbose output only with
  `localStorage.setItem('auh_debug','1')`.
- **No build step, no framework, no bundler.** New files are plain scripts added
  to the ordered list in `index.html` (and copied by the Pages workflow, which
  copies the whole `js/` directory).

================================================================
FILE: DATA-MODEL.md
================================================================
# Data Model — what the app reads and what it computes

All displayed content comes from **public Google Sheets**, read live in the
browser through Google's **gviz** endpoint. There is no server and no write path.

> **Column-level detail lives in [docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md](docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md)**,
> which is generated from `js/data/schema.js` and therefore can never drift from
> the code. Regenerate it with `node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs`.
> This file covers the parts that are *not* a column list: how columns are
> resolved, what the app computes itself, and how caching works.

---

## 1. Sources

| Schema key | Spreadsheet | GID | Fetched as | Content |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `residents` | main | `0` | CSV | لائحة المقيمين |
| `oncall` | main | `238974679` | JSON | المناوبات (السنة الأولى) |
| `oncallYear2` | year2 | `0` | CSV | مناوبات السنة الثانية (spreadsheet `1dOvCHFQBYz0wFklUFicjf8iU3IscJNzUrUcSYeKMlh8`) |
| `oncallAdjustments` | main | `1181737768` | CSV | تعديل الساعات والبونص |
| `evaluation` | main | `253629565` | CSV | التقييم السنوي |
| `links` | main | `1649404909` | CSV | روابط هامة |
| `qa` | main | `680270268` | JSON | الأسئلة والأجوبة |
| `lectures` | main | `393274093` | CSV | رزنامة المحاضرات |
| `holidays` | main | `1388329552` | CSV | العطل الرسمية (تاريخ + اسم) |
| `oncallRules` | main | `1364488029` | CSV | العطل السنوية (تبويب قديم، ما زال يُدمج) |

Main spreadsheet: `1Pb5VK1HsccaJpKXm-jersktd8yk4jf1V7o8qsDDmCI4`.
Both spreadsheets must stay shared as **"anyone with the link can view"**.

Both endpoints are normalized to `[headerRow, ...dataRows]` (arrays of strings),
so parsers never care which transport was used.

**Two transports.** Google echoes the page's `Origin` in its CORS header, so a
page opened straight from disk (`file://`, origin `null`) gets no CORS header and
every `fetch` fails silently. The app therefore falls back to a JSONP
(`<script>`) request, which CORS does not apply to — both paths produce identical
tables. `AUH.config.sheetsTransport` (`auto` | `fetch` | `jsonp`) forces one.
`AUH.config.dataSource` (`auto` | `gviz` | `api`) chooses the sheets or a future
JSON backend; `auto` prefers the backend when `apiBaseUrl` is set and falls back
to the sheets.

---

## 2. How columns are found (no fixed positions)

Every field is declared in `js/data/schema.js` with the header names it may
appear under, and resolved by `js/data/header-map.js`:

1. exact match (Arabic-normalized: hamza/ta-marbuta/"ال"/spacing ignored)
2. prefix match — for headers carrying a weight, e.g. `المهارات السريرية 25%`
3. contains match — **only** when exactly one unclaimed header matches
4. a declared fallback position — only for columns the sheet leaves unlabeled

Repeated columns are discovered by pattern, so **adding a month needs no code
change**: `فرز شهر N` (rotation) and `مناوبات شهر N` (that month's on-call count).

Anything unresolved is reported: `console.error` for a required field,
`console.warn` for an optional one, and in `node tools/verify-data.mjs`.

**On-call category columns** are likewise discovered by header: every column that
is neither the day nor the date column and has a non-empty header is a duty
category. Adding, renaming or moving a category in the sheet is safe.

---

## 3. Canonical on-call shape

Both years are normalized to the same structure regardless of their sheet layout:

```
headers: ['اليوم', 'التاريخ', ...categoryNames]
rows:    [{ date: 'YYYY-MM-DD', day: 'الأحد', row: [dayName, isoDate, ...cells] }]
```

The Year-2 sheet has been published in two different layouts:

- **one header row** with the label repeated per column (current), or
- **two merged header rows** (group + sub-role, only meaningful inside "الاسعاف").

`parseOncallYear2` detects which by testing whether row 1 starts with a date, and
forward-fills merged cells. Dates there use a backslash separator (`1\8\2026`),
which `extractDate()` accepts along with `/`, `-` and `.`.

`YEAR2_CATEGORY_MAP` (in `js/domain/oncall-schedule.js`) maps a Year-1 category to
its Year-2 equivalent(s) for the "زملاء السنة الثانية" line in معلوماتي. Matching
is Arabic-normalized, so `الديال` ↔ `ديال` needs no entry of its own.

---

## 4. Evaluation and praises (الثناءات)

- Real rows are identified by **content**, not position: any row with a name that
  is not the `مثال توضيحي` demo row. The sheet's trailing totals row (no name) is
  ignored automatically.
- **`الثناءات` / `العقوبات` are counts today** (`2`, `5`, …). The parser reads a
  numeric cell as that number; if the cell is free text instead, it counts one
  entry per line. `-` or empty is 0.
  *(Before 2026-08-13 the app only counted line breaks, so every praised resident
  scored 1 in احصائيات الأطباء while التقييم السنوي showed the real number.)*
- **Matching a resident to their evaluation row** is deliberately strict, in
  order: full name → unique abbreviation → unique `exactNameMatch` → unique
  token-set match → unique word-subset match. A duplicated abbreviation (it
  happens: two people abbreviated `السيد`) is excluded from the abbreviation
  index so nobody inherits someone else's praises.

---

## 5. Computed doctor statistics

There is no statistics tab in any sheet. `computeDoctorStats()`
(`js/domain/doctor-stats.js`) builds one entry per resident by walking the
Year-1 on-call log:

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `total` / `completed` / `remaining` | assignments, split by date vs. today |
| `wards` / `icu` / `emergency` / `misc` | via `classifyGroup(category)` |
| `groupDetails[group][category]` | per-category counts inside each group |
| `catDates[category]` | the actual dates behind each count |
| `holiday` / `night` | Fri/Sat + annual holidays; categories containing "ليلي" |
| `hoursCompleted` / `hoursTotal` | summed duty durations (past / all) |
| `rankCompleted` / `rankTotal` | rank by those hours |
| `firstOncall` / `lastOncall` | earliest/latest on-call date |
| `praiseCount` / `penaltyCount` | from the evaluation sheet (see above) |
| `rotations` / `rotationsCount` | `فرز شهر N` values up to the current month |
| `status` / `isDetachedOrNotJoined` | drives the red-tinted card + badge |

Group classification: **أجنحة** تاني/ثاني، تالت/ثالث، رابع، خارجيات، سابع ·
**عنايات** anything containing عناية · **اسعاف** anything starting with اسعاف/إسعاف ·
**منوع** أورام، ديال.

Someone listed in the on-call sheet but missing from the roster still gets an
entry (never silently dropped) — `tools/verify-data.mjs` flags those names.

The roster's "المناوبات" column shows this computed `total`
(`getComputedCumulativeOncalls`), not a hand-maintained cell; the sheet's old
`المناوبات+` column no longer exists and is optional in the schema.

---

## 6. Duty times and durations (fixed in code)

`js/domain/oncall-schedule.js` holds two tables and the date duty changed from
partial to full shifts:

- `SWITCH_DATE = '2026-07-23'` — compared against **the on-call day**, not today.
- `ONCALL_SCHEDULE_OLD` — days before it (شفتات جزئية).
- `ONCALL_SCHEDULE_NEW` — days on/after it (شفتات كاملة).

Each entry has `workTime`, `workDuration`, `holidayTime`, `holidayDuration` as
Arabic strings shown as-is; hours are parsed out of the duration text
("7 ساعات ونصف" → 7.5). `getCategorySchedule()` picks the table by date, matches
the category by normalized name (with an emergency-prefix fallback), then picks
the holiday or workday row.

### What counts as a holiday

A day is a holiday when it is **Friday or Saturday**, or when it appears in the
**العطل الرسمية** tab (`GID 1388329552`) — two columns, `التاريخ` (day-month-year)
and `اسم العطلة`. Dates from the old `العطل السنوية` section of the rules tab are
still merged in for backward compatibility.

`app.applyDataset()` merges both into `this.annualHolidays`, which is the single
`Set` every holiday-aware code path reads, so **adding one row to that sheet**
automatically changes:

- the duty **time and duration** for that day (`getCategorySchedule` picks the
  holiday row) — and therefore every computed hour figure and ranking;
- the `holiday` counter in the doctor statistics;
- the colouring of both calendars (a named holiday gets its own red/gold cell,
  a star and the holiday's name) and the badge in the on-call day card;
- the "العطل الرسمية" panel in معلوماتي and the exported PNG calendar.

`AUH.parse.holidays` also exposes `nameFor(date)`, `inMonth('YYYY-MM')` and
`upcoming(fromDate, limit)` for the UI.

---

## 7. Hour adjustments and bonus hours

The `oncallAdjustments` tab (`GID 1181737768`) carries three different things,
told apart by its own content — columns are: name, abbreviation, date, type, hours.

**Shift rows** (a real date + a duty category) resolve into exactly one of:

- **override** — the person is already listed for that date+category, so their
  hours replace the standard duration (badge "ساعات معدّلة").
- **addition** — they are not listed: a volunteer/extra shift, treated everywhere
  as a real assignment (badge "تطوعي", gold dot on the month calendar).

**Bonus rows** — the date **or** the type cell says `Bonus` (also accepted:
`بونص`, `بونس`, `مكافأة`):

| Date cell | Type cell | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| a real date | `Bonus` | bonus hours belonging to that month |
| `Bonus` | `Bonus` | undated bonus hours (totals only) |

Bonus hours are credited to the person **without creating an on-call
assignment**: the shift count, group breakdown, holiday/night counters and the
calendars are untouched. They are added to `hoursTotal` (and to `hoursCompleted`
when undated or already past), so they do affect the hours ranking.

`computeDoctorStats()` exposes them as `bonusHours`, `bonusCompleted`,
`bonusByMonth` (`{'YYYY-MM': hours}`) and `bonusEntries`, alongside
`hoursShiftsTotal` / `hoursShiftsCompleted` (the same figures without bonus), so
every card can show "منها X بونص" and stay traceable.

Consumed by `computeDoctorStats`, the statistics cards, "معلوماتي" (top summary,
a per-month card and a dedicated ساعات البونص section) and the Excel export.
Not applied to the raw "عرض كجدول" table, which mirrors the sheet literally.
Only Year-1 gets adjustments.

## 8. Caching & refresh

- Raw tables are cached in `localStorage` under **`hc_v64_<buildId>`**
  (`AUH.config.cacheKey`) with a timestamp; entries older than **10 minutes**
  are ignored. An **empty payload is never written and never read** — a failed
  load must not poison the next visit with a blank site.
- All storage access goes through `AUH.storage`, which degrades to memory when
  the browser forbids it (private mode, blocked cookies) instead of throwing.
- Background refresh every **180 s** while visible, plus on tab re-focus, plus
  the manual refresh button. A refresh whose content signature matches the
  current data is a no-op (nothing re-renders); when it differs, the visitor's
  tab, selected on-call date, month and scroll position are preserved and a red
  "New data" badge invites an explicit refresh.
- Every fetch uses `cache: 'no-store'` and a cache-busting query parameter.
- Bump `cacheVersion` in `js/core/config.js` to invalidate every visitor's cache
  after a breaking data change.

---

## 9. Known sheet-side issues (2026-08-13)

Found by `node tools/verify-data.mjs` — these are **data** problems, not code:

1. Year-2 on-call sheet: the last row is dated `31\8\2027` (should be 2026), so
   that day is parsed into 2027 and disappears from August.
2. `محمد وليد حنكي` has no row in the evaluation sheet (praises show as 0).
3. `محمد يمان اسامة دباغ درويش` is written as `اسامة محمديمان دباغ درويش` with a
   different abbreviation in the evaluation sheet; the app still matches him via
   its last-resort unique-match rule, but the two sheets should agree.

================================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/pages.yml
================================================================
name: pages build and deployment

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  pages: write
  id-token: write

concurrency:
  group: pages
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Pages
        uses: actions/configure-pages@v5

      - name: Prepare static site
        run: |
          mkdir -p _site
          BUILD_ID="${GITHUB_SHA::12}"
          sed "s/__BUILD_ID__/${BUILD_ID}/g" index.html > _site/index.html
          cp styles.css bg.png bg.webp bg.webp.png _site/
          cp -R js _site/js
          touch _site/.nojekyll

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-pages-artifact@v3
        with:
          path: _site

  deploy:
    environment:
      name: github-pages
      url: ${{ steps.deployment_1.outputs.page_url || steps.deployment_2.outputs.page_url || steps.deployment_3.outputs.page_url }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build
    timeout-minutes: 20
    steps:
      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages (attempt 1)
        id: deployment_1
        continue-on-error: true
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4

      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages (attempt 2)
        id: deployment_2
        if: ${{ steps.deployment_1.outcome == 'failure' }}
        continue-on-error: true
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4

      - name: Deploy to GitHub Pages (attempt 3)
        id: deployment_3
        if: ${{ steps.deployment_1.outcome == 'failure' && steps.deployment_2.outcome == 'failure' }}
        continue-on-error: true
        uses: actions/deploy-pages@v4

      - name: Fail workflow if all deploy attempts failed
        if: ${{ steps.deployment_1.outcome == 'failure' && steps.deployment_2.outcome == 'failure' && steps.deployment_3.outcome == 'failure' }}
        run: |
          echo "All GitHub Pages deploy attempts failed."
          exit 1

================================================================
FILE: docs/BACKEND-MIGRATION.md
================================================================
# خطة التحويل إلى موقع ديناميكي (سيرفر + قاعدة بيانات)

هذا المستند يشرح **أين بالضبط** يُوصل السيرفر بالكود الحالي، وما الذي لا يحتاج
تعديلاً أصلاً. البنية الحالية رُتّبت خصيصاً لهذا الغرض: طبقة البيانات معزولة
خلف واجهة واحدة، وكل المنطق (الإحصائيات، جداول الدوام، الثناءات، الفروز) لا يعرف
شيئاً عن Google Sheets.

---

## 1. الطبقات الحالية

```
index.html
└── js/
    ├── core/      إعدادات + دوال نصية/تاريخية نقية (لا DOM ولا بيانات)
    ├── data/      عقد البيانات: schema → header-map → transport → parsers → repository
    ├── domain/    قواعد العمل: جداول الدوام، العطل، التعديلات، إحصائيات الأطباء
    ├── views/     العرض فقط (تُدمج في HospitalApp.prototype)
    └── app.js     المنسّق: دورة التحميل + واجهة رقيقة تستدعيها الـ views
```

القاعدة: **كل طبقة تستعمل ما فوقها فقط.** لا يستدعي أي view واجهة Google مباشرة،
ولا يعرف أي parser شيئاً عن الـ DOM.

---

## 2. نقطة الوصل الوحيدة

كل الجلب يمرّ عبر ملفين:

| الملف | الدور |
|---|---|
| `js/data/gviz-client.js` | النقل من Google Sheets: fetch مع بديل JSONP (يعمل حتى مع فتح الملف مباشرة) |
| `js/data/api-client.js` | النقل من السيرفر (جاهز، خامل حتى تُضبط `apiBaseUrl`) |
| `js/data/repository.js` | التنسيق: يجلب كل المصادر → `parseAll()` → `dataset` + الكاش |

وفي `js/core/config.js`:

```js
dataSource: 'auto',       // 'auto' | 'gviz' | 'api'
apiBaseUrl: '',           // اضبطه ليتحول الموقع إلى السيرفر
sheetsTransport: 'auto',  // 'auto' | 'fetch' | 'jsonp'
```

`repository.clientFor()` هو المكان الوحيد الذي يتفرّع على `dataSource`، و
`repository.fetchAll()` هو المكان الوحيد الذي يعرف قائمة المصادر.

---

## 3. أبسط طريق للتحويل (مرحلتان)

### المرحلة أ — سيرفر يعيد نفس الشكل الحالي (جاهزة مسبقاً — سطر واحد)

`js/data/api-client.js` **مكتوب وموجود بالفعل**، ولا يفعل شيئاً حتى تضبط العنوان:

```js
// js/core/config.js
apiBaseUrl: 'https://api.example.com',   // ← هذا كل ما يلزم
dataSource: 'auto',                      // الافتراضي: API إن وُجد، وإلا Google Sheets
```

ما الذي يتوقعه من السيرفر:

```
GET {apiBaseUrl}/tables/{sourceKey}     مثال: /tables/residents
->  [["ت","الاسم الثلاثي", …], ["1","رزان …", …]]        (صف العناوين أولاً)
    أو  {"headers":[…], "rows":[[…], …]}
```

`sourceKey` هو مفتاح المصدر في الـ schema: `residents`, `oncall`, `oncallYear2`,
`oncallAdjustments`, `evaluation`, `links`, `qa`, `lectures`, `oncallRules`.

مع `dataSource: 'auto'` يبقى Google Sheets شبكة أمان: إذا تعطّل السيرفر أو لم
يستجب، يعود الموقع تلقائياً للشيتات بدل أن يفرغ من البيانات. لذلك يمكن تشغيل
السيرفر تدريجياً دون أي مخاطرة على المستخدمين.

### المرحلة ب — سيرفر يعيد سجلات JSON نظيفة (الشكل النهائي)

عندما تصبح البيانات في قاعدة بيانات فعلية، الأفضل أن يعيد السيرفر سجلات مباشرة
بدل جداول. عندها يُضاف تفرّع واحد في `repository.parseAll()`:

```js
const residents = raw.residents
  ? (AUH.config.dataSource === 'api'
      ? AUH.parse.residentsFromRecords(raw.residents)   // يبني نفس الـ model
      : AUH.parse.residents(raw.residents))
  : prev.residents;
```

المهم أن يبقى **شكل الـ model** كما هو (نفس الحقول ونفس الدوال)، لأن كل ما فوقه
مبني عليه. الحقول المطلوبة موجودة في `js/data/parsers/*.js` وموثّقة في
[DATA-CONTRACT.md](DATA-CONTRACT.md).

---

## 4. مقترح مخطط قاعدة البيانات

الأسماء مطابقة لحقول الـ schema الحالية حتى تبقى الترجمة مباشرة.

```sql
-- المقيمون
CREATE TABLE residents (
  id            SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  seq           INT,                 -- الترتيب المعروض
  name          TEXT NOT NULL,       -- الاسم الثلاثي
  abbr          TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,-- الاختصار (مفتاح الربط)
  phone         TEXT,
  gender        TEXT,
  specialty     TEXT,                -- الاختصاص
  university    TEXT,
  join_date     DATE,
  status        TEXT NOT NULL,       -- تم الالتحاق / تم الانفكاك / ...
  detach_date   DATE,
  detach_reason TEXT,
  created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
);

-- الفروز الشهرية (بدل أعمدة "فرز شهر N")
CREATE TABLE rotations (
  resident_id INT REFERENCES residents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  year        INT NOT NULL,
  month       INT NOT NULL CHECK (month BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
  rotation    TEXT NOT NULL,         -- اسم الفرز
  PRIMARY KEY (resident_id, year, month)
);

-- فئات المناوبات (بدل عناوين الأعمدة)
CREATE TABLE oncall_categories (
  id       SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  name     TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,     -- "عناية قلبية" ...
  grp      TEXT NOT NULL,            -- wards | icu | emergency | misc
  is_night BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  year_level INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1  -- 1 أو 2
);

-- المناوبات (كل سطر = شخص + يوم + فئة)
CREATE TABLE oncall_shifts (
  id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  shift_date  DATE NOT NULL,
  category_id INT REFERENCES oncall_categories(id),
  resident_id INT REFERENCES residents(id),
  hours       NUMERIC(4,1),          -- NULL = استخدم المدة القياسية
  kind        TEXT DEFAULT 'regular',-- regular | volunteer
  note        TEXT,
  UNIQUE (shift_date, category_id, resident_id)
);

-- التقييم السنوي
CREATE TABLE evaluations (
  resident_id INT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES residents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  academic_year TEXT,
  scores      JSONB,                 -- {clinical: 70, knowledge: 80, ...}
  total       NUMERIC(5,2),
  praise_count   INT DEFAULT 0,      -- عدد الثناءات
  penalty_count  INT DEFAULT 0
);

-- تفاصيل الثناءات/العقوبات (اختياري لكنه الأنظف)
CREATE TABLE evaluation_notes (
  id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  resident_id INT REFERENCES residents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  kind        TEXT NOT NULL,         -- praise | penalty
  note        TEXT,
  noted_on    DATE,
  created_by  TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE lectures (...);   -- نفس حقول شيت المحاضرات
CREATE TABLE links (...);
CREATE TABLE qa (...);
CREATE TABLE holidays (holiday_date DATE PRIMARY KEY, label TEXT);
```

> ملاحظة مهمة عن الثناءات: في الشيت اليوم العمود رقم مجرّد. جدول
> `evaluation_notes` يجعل الرقم **محسوباً** (`COUNT(*)`) بدل أن يُكتب يدوياً،
> فتختفي أخطاء العدّ نهائياً. الواجهة تستعمل `praiseCount` كما هي.

---

## 5. مقترح واجهة REST

```
GET  /api/residents                  → [{id, name, abbr, phone, specialty, status, joinDate, rotations:[{month, rotation}]}]
GET  /api/oncall?from=&to=&year=1    → [{date, category, residents:[abbr], hours?}]
GET  /api/evaluations                → [{residentId, scores, total, praiseCount, penaltyCount}]
GET  /api/lectures?from=             → [{date, title, speaker, place, time, duration, dept, year, links}]
GET  /api/links | /api/qa | /api/holidays
GET  /api/snapshot                   → كل ما سبق في طلب واحد (يوازي loadFresh الحالي)
```

`/api/snapshot` هو الأقرب لسلوك الموقع الحالي (طلب واحد لكل شيء + كاش محلي)،
ويقلّل زمن التحميل الأول.

للكتابة لاحقاً (لوحة إدارة):

```
POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/residents/:id, /api/oncall/:id, /api/evaluations/:id
POST /api/oncall/bulk        ← استيراد شهر كامل دفعة واحدة
```

مع مصادقة (JWT أو جلسة) وصلاحيات: **قراءة عامة**، وكتابة لرئاسة المقيمين فقط.

---

## 6. ما الذي لا يتغيّر إطلاقاً

- `js/domain/*` — الإحصائيات وجداول الدوام والعطل والتعديلات: دوال نقية تأخذ
  models وتُعيد نتائج. تعمل كما هي فوق أي مصدر بيانات (بل وتعمل في Node، وهو ما
  تستفيد منه `tools/verify-data.mjs`).
- `js/views/*` — تعرض من الـ models فقط.
- `js/core/*` — التوحيد العربي والبحث والتواريخ.
- شكل الكاش المحلي (`localStorage`) — يبقى مفيداً للعمل السريع/شبه دون اتصال.

---

## 7. خطوات مقترحة بالترتيب

1. **قاعدة بيانات + استيراد أولي** من الشيتات الحالية (سكربت يستخدم نفس الـ parsers
   في `tools/` — فهي تعمل في Node أصلاً).
2. **سيرفر قراءة فقط** يخدم `/api/snapshot` بنفس شكل الجداول (المرحلة أ) →
   تحويل `dataSource: 'api'` واختبار الموقع كما هو.
3. **مزامنة مؤقتة**: تبقى الشيتات مصدر التحرير، والسيرفر يستوردها دورياً، حتى
   تجهز لوحة الإدارة.
4. **لوحة إدارة للكتابة** (مصادقة + صلاحيات + سجل تعديلات) ثم إيقاف التحرير على
   الشيتات.
5. **حذف `gviz-client.js`** ومصادر الشيتات من `schema` عند الاستغناء عنها.

---

## 8. تنبيهات

- **الاختصار (`abbr`) هو مفتاح الربط** في كل مكان اليوم. في قاعدة البيانات اجعل
  الربط عبر `resident_id`، وأبقِ `abbr` فريداً للعرض والاستيراد.
- **التواريخ**: التطبيق يتعامل مع `YYYY-MM-DD` نصاً في كل مكان. اجعل الـ API
  يُخرج التواريخ بهذه الصيغة (لا ISO كامل بتوقيت) لتفادي انزياح يوم كامل.
- **الساعات**: `hours` في `oncall_shifts` تقابل «تعديلات المناوبات» الحالية
  (تجاوز المدة القياسية). اتركها `NULL` للمناوبة العادية.
- **الأمان**: الشيت العام اليوم يعني أن أرقام هواتف المقيمين متاحة لمن يملك
  الرابط. عند الانتقال إلى سيرفر، هذه فرصة لإخفاء الأرقام خلف تسجيل دخول.

FILE: docs/DATA-CONTRACT.md
================================================================
# عقد البيانات — أعمدة جداول Google Sheets

> ⚠️ **هذا الملف مولَّد آلياً من `js/data/schema.js`.**
> لا تعدّله يدوياً؛ عدّل الـ schema ثم شغّل:

```bash
node tools/generate-data-contract.mjs
```

---

## كيف يقرأ الموقع الأعمدة؟

لا يعتمد الموقع على **ترتيب** الأعمدة، بل على **اسم العمود** في صف العناوين.
يمكن تحريك عمود أو إضافة عمود جديد بينها دون أي تعديل على الكود، بشرط بقاء الاسم كما هو.

ترتيب المطابقة:

1. **تطابق تام** بعد التوحيد (تجاهل الهمزات، التاء المربوطة، «ال» التعريف، والمسافات الزائدة).
2. **تطابق بادئة** — مفيد لعناوين تحمل نسبة مئوية مثل `المهارات السريرية 25%`.
3. **تطابق جزئي** — يُقبل فقط إذا كان هناك عمود واحد مطابق (لا تخمين عند التعدد).
4. **الموضع الافتراضي** — فقط للأعمدة التي لا عنوان لها أصلاً في الشيت (مثل عمود «ت»).

إذا تعذّر إيجاد عمود **إلزامي**، يظهر خطأ واضح في الـ console وفي أداة الفحص
(`node tools/verify-data.mjs`) بدل أن تظهر أرقام خاطئة بصمت.

**قواعد مهمة لمن يحرّر الشيت:**

- لا تُعِد تسمية عمود إلزامي (الاسم/الاختصار/الحالة/التاريخ...) دون تحديث الـ schema.
- الاختصار (`الاختصار`) هو مفتاح الربط بين اللوائح وجداول المناوبات — يجب أن يبقى فريداً.
- الأعمدة الشهرية تُكتشف تلقائياً بنمط `فرز شهر N` و `مناوبات شهر N`.
- صفوف بلا اسم (كصف المجاميع في نهاية شيت التقييم) تُتجاهَل تلقائياً.

---

## أوقات ومدد المناوبات

ليست في الشيت: هي ثابتة في `js/domain/oncall-schedule.js` (جدولان: قبل وبعد
`2026-07-23` تاريخ التحول من الشفتات الجزئية إلى الكاملة). لتعديل توقيت أو مدة،
عدّل ذلك الملف مباشرة.

## التحقق

```bash
node tools/verify-data.mjs         # فحص شامل مقابل الشيتات الحقيقية
node tools/verify-data.mjs --debug # مع طباعة خريطة الأعمدة المكتشفة
```
